# Agents · Q · part 3

957 entries, qp-security-reviewer to quota-budget-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **qp-security-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for the quality-pass security & data-integrity dimension — authz gaps, tenant/owner scoping, enumeration, secrets in responses, unbounded pagination, private-data egress. Use during quality-pass Phase 1. ([Telli/quality-pass-plugin](https://github.com/Telli/quality-pass-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/qp-security-reviewer.md))
- **qp-slop-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for the quality-pass slop & honesty dimension — fabricated stats, placeholder copy, fake UI state, brand/icon/theme inconsistency. Use during quality-pass Phase 1. ([Telli/quality-pass-plugin](https://github.com/Telli/quality-pass-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/qp-slop-reviewer.md))
- **qpr-macro**: Macro research agent for the QPR auto-fill workflow. Researches GDP, FX, and private consumption for the 5 SEA-5 countries (Indonesia, Thailand, Philippines, Malaysia, Vietnam) from primary statistical agencies (BPS, NESDC, PSA, DOSM, GSO, BI, BOT). ([ACV-Capital/qpr-autofill](https://github.com/ACV-Capital/qpr-autofill/blob/HEAD/agents/qpr-macro.md))
- **qpr-sssg**: SSSG research agent for the QPR auto-fill workflow. Researches same-store sales growth (YoY %) for ~20 retail tickers across SEA-5 (Indonesia, Thailand, Philippines, Malaysia, Vietnam) from company IR pages, MD&A, and respected business news. ([ACV-Capital/qpr-autofill](https://github.com/ACV-Capital/qpr-autofill/blob/HEAD/agents/qpr-sssg.md))
- **qralph-team-lead**: Sub-team lead for QRALPH hierarchical orchestration - manages agents within a single context window ([sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills](https://github.com/sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/qralph/agents/qralph-team-lead.md))
- **qralph-validator**: Fresh-context validation agent for QRALPH - runs UAT against requirements independently ([sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills](https://github.com/sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/qralph/agents/qralph-validator.md))

- **quest-executor**: Works exactly one quest record iteratively per the quest protocol until complete or blocked, recording checkpoint evidence via the quest CLI. - **quest-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer for quests claiming complete — verifies the checkpoint evidence actually discharges every Done-when item and hunts silent substitutions, fake greens, and scope drift. Dispatch before accepting any non-trivial complete ruling. &lt;example&gt;Executor checkpoints quest 12 complete → orchestrator dispatches quest-reviewer on the diff + record; it re-runs the validation loop and reports findings with required dispositions.&lt;/example&gt; ([robertsreberski/quest](https://github.com/robertsreberski/quest/blob/HEAD/agents/quest-reviewer.md))

- **questioner**: Use as the first agent of the QRSPI pipeline. Decomposes a user's task description into a full task record (1-task.md) and neutral research questions (2-questions.md), plus conditional artifacts — a 3-prd.md when the PRD criteria apply, and a 4-repos.md listing the repos the topic touches when the description names more than one repository. The researcher who reads 2-questions.md should have no idea what feature is being built. ([bostonaholic/team](https://github.com/bostonaholic/team/blob/HEAD/agents/questioner.md) · ★11)
- **questioner**: QRSPI stage Q. Turns a vague feature request into a concrete list of technical questions the codebase must answer. Writes openspec/changes/&lt;id&gt;/questions.md. ([lotea-be/qrspi](https://github.com/lotea-be/qrspi/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/questioner.md) · ★2)
- **questioner**: Asks the verification questions that the decision-maker would skip under pressure. Does NOT diagnose biases - poses the questions that surface them. - **quiz-question-generator**: Especialista em gerar perguntas de quiz baseadas em chunks de banco RAG. Use quando precisar gerar perguntas específicas, detalhadas e sem duplicatas a partir de conteúdo estruturado em chunks. ([diegofornalha/plugin-rag-to-quiz](https://github.com/diegofornalha/plugin-rag-to-quiz/blob/HEAD/plugins/gerar-quiz-rag/agents/quiz-question-generator.md))
- **quiz-taker**: The teach-back-verification comprehension gate. A fresh-context reader that answers a quiz about a shipped change using ONLY the report baked into its prompt — no code access, no session memory. Use when the task-to-pr Stage 5 gate (FULL tier) or the teach-back-verification skill dispatches the quiz subagent. Never for exploration or implementation. ([cang-pham-codeleap/jtl-component-lib-plugin](https://github.com/cang-pham-codeleap/jtl-component-lib-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/comp-lib-process/agents/quiz-taker.md))
- **quiz-writer**: Writes or revises a module's assessment quiz (NN-quiz.md) from its finished lesson content. Use once a module's numbered lesson files are drafted and it needs an assessment before it's ready for internal review. ([dhigby/virtual-ltct](https://github.com/dhigby/virtual-ltct/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quiz-writer.md))
- **quizmaster**: Quizzes the learner on the agentic-engineering guide in a fresh context, so the just-given explanation can't leak into the questions. Use at the end of each module for a quick check, and at the end of each tier for a 5-question mixed quiz. ([krivitsky/professional-agentic-product-engineering](https://github.com/krivitsky/professional-agentic-product-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quizmaster.md) · ★22)
- **quizmaster**: 자바 문법 퀴즈, 스프링 개념 확인 질문, 구현 과제를 출제할 때 사용한다. "과제 줘", "문제 내줘", "퀴즈", "뭐 만들어볼까". 뼈대 없이 요구사항과 검증 기준만 준다. ([songjihwanimnida/spring-studyon](https://github.com/songjihwanimnida/spring-studyon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quizmaster.md))
- **quizzes**: This returns all quizzes with: title, allowed_attempts, quiz_type, description (HTML), published, time_limit, points_possible. ([dchandarana07/claude-plugins](https://github.com/dchandarana07/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/course-qa/skills/qa-checklist/agents/quizzes.md))
- **quorum-architecture**: Decides which parts of the system change and how they talk — components touched, data and contract deltas, boundaries, failure modes. Skipped at complexity simple. Names deltas against the profile's component map, never a stack it assumed. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-architecture.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-audit**: The governance auditor. Runs at both gates — the scope audit before implementation and the implementation audit after it. Deterministic, blocker-first, evidence-based. Convenes a critic panel, emits a proposal for every non-clean verdict, and never fixes anything itself. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-audit.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-auditor**: Measures a repository against numbered spec criteria and returns a status for each. Reads and searches code; runs nothing and cannot modify anything. ([AiFirstDevelopment/quorum](https://github.com/AiFirstDevelopment/quorum/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum/agents/quorum-auditor.md))
- **quorum-builder**: Implements an approved quorum plan, ticking off steps and recording deviations. Used by the autonomous pipeline. ([AiFirstDevelopment/quorum](https://github.com/AiFirstDevelopment/quorum/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum/agents/quorum-builder.md))
- **quorum-decision-documenter**: Records architectural and technical decisions as ADRs in the consumer repo's memory bank. Stack-agnostic. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-decision-documenter.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-delivery**: The last stage. Opens the PR, posts the handoff, updates the tracker, and moves the unit to MERGED. Runs only on a VERIFIED-family verdict, and never automatically — delivery acts outward, so it stops for a human in both autonomy modes. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-delivery.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-design**: Works out how each component change behaves — flows, contracts in full, schema and indexes, edge cases, states. The last stage before tasks, and the one where the edge cases have to surface. Skipped at complexity simple. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-design.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-judge**: Adjudicates review findings against the plan and the code, applies accepted fixes, and writes the verdict. Used by the autonomous pipeline. ([AiFirstDevelopment/quorum](https://github.com/AiFirstDevelopment/quorum/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum/agents/quorum-judge.md))
- **quorum-manager**: Use for managing quorum rules and voting thresholds in distributed work. ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/agents/quorum-manager.md) · ★31)
- **quorum-manager**: Runs vote tallies over subagent votes and manages membership thresholds for monomind's single-process consensus primitives ([monoes/monomind](https://github.com/monoes/monomind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quorum-manager.md) · ★21 · 2 copies)
- **quorum-manager**: Consensus gate for destructive operations. Before any agent executes a destructive action (drop table, force push, delete branch, truncate data, remove migration), it must check with quorum-manager. Requires majority approval (2/3 agents or explicit human approval) before allowing the action. Stores approvals in .autopilot/consensus.json. ([Nerfherder16/BrickLayer](https://github.com/Nerfherder16/BrickLayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quorum-manager.md))
- **quorum-manager**: Implements dynamic quorum adjustment and intelligent membership management ([storesuper22-cyber/sutazaiapp](https://github.com/storesuper22-cyber/sutazaiapp/blob/HEAD/archives/agents/quorum-manager.md) · 2 copies)
- **quorum-memory-synchronizer**: Keeps the consumer repo's memory bank synchronized with the codebase. Updates patterns, decisions, and architecture docs after implementation. Archives obsolete entries. Stack-agnostic. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-memory-synchronizer.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-orchestrator**: End-to-end ticket delivery orchestrator. Stack-agnostic. Reads `.claude/profile.yml` in the consumer repo to learn which skills/commands/paths apply, then chains the right agents and gates the work behind human approvals at every phase. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-pattern-documenter**: Documents reusable code patterns in the consumer repo's memory bank with real examples extracted from the codebase. Stack-agnostic. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-pattern-documenter.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-pr-generator**: Generates PR descriptions linking the ticket, investigation prompt, validation report, and artifact summary. Stack-agnostic — adapts run commands, related-artifact tables, and the testing checklist to the consumer's profile. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-pr-generator.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-prd**: Shapes the problem before anyone shapes a solution. Interviews the operator, writes prd.md, and stops — this stage is always human-interactive in both autonomy modes, because an agent that writes the PRD defines the problem and the solution at once. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-prd.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-prompt-builder**: Builds comprehensive 6-section investigation prompts from ticket analysis and skill recommendations. Integrates image analysis specs when available. Saves to .claude/prompts/{TICKET-KEY}-{DATE}.md. Stack-agnostic. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-prompt-builder.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-publisher**: Pushes the branch and opens (or updates) the pull request or merge request that presents the finished work. Reads and runs git; cannot edit code. ([AiFirstDevelopment/quorum](https://github.com/AiFirstDevelopment/quorum/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum/agents/quorum-publisher.md))
- **quorum-qa-handoff-publisher**: Publishes the Dev→QA handoff package at Phase 7 of the orchestrator pipeline. Builds an automated-test-coverage markdown and creates a subtask on the ticket "Review Automation Tests" under the story whose description is the FULL markdown content (QA has no repo access). Manual QA cases are deliberately excluded to avoid biasing QA's own analysis. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-qa-handoff-publisher.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-review-loop**: **RULE: After pushing fixes for a `quorum` review, the reviewer MUST be re-requested — removed and added again. A fix round that ends without that re-request is not finished.** ([moto-nrw/project-phoenix](https://github.com/moto-nrw/project-phoenix/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/quorum-review-loop.md) · ★9)
- **quorum-reviewer**: Reviews a diff through one assigned lens and returns structured findings. Reads code but cannot modify it. ([AiFirstDevelopment/quorum](https://github.com/AiFirstDevelopment/quorum/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum/agents/quorum-reviewer.md))
- **quorum-scribe**: Transcribes structured review findings into review files under docs/work/&lt;slug&gt;/reviews/. Write-only; cannot read or alter source code. ([AiFirstDevelopment/quorum](https://github.com/AiFirstDevelopment/quorum/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum/agents/quorum-scribe.md))
- **quorum-spec**: Turns an approved PRD or a ticket analysis into requirements.md — goals, scope, non-goals, constraints, and acceptance criteria that a later audit can trace tasks back to. Records decisions; never invents them. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-spec.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-tasks**: Turns the approved scope into an ordered task list where every task carries its file set and cites the acceptance criterion it serves. Both origins converge here, and the file sets are what the executor and the batch queue both read. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-tasks.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-test-specialist**: Plans test strategy for a ticket and delegates file scaffolding to the consumer repo's stack-specific generator skills. Stack-agnostic — reads `.claude/profile.yml` to learn which generators to invoke for unit / integration / E2E tiers. Two modes: plan (decide what to test, where, and with what assertions) and execute (delegate to the generator skill). ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-test-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-ticket-analyzer**: Extracts structured implementation/test-generation inputs (routes, roles, complexity, affected surfaces) from a ticket. Stack-agnostic — adapts surface extraction to the consumer's profile. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-ticket-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-ticket-image-analyzer**: Downloads and visually analyzes images attached to a ticket, fetched through the configured tracker role. Extracts UI specs, text content, styling details, and component states from screenshots. Flags conflicts between images and text description. Produces structured analysis saved to .claude/prompts/images/&lt;slug&gt;/analysis.md. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-ticket-image-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-ticket-validator**: Validates implementation artifacts against a ticket's acceptance criteria. Stack-agnostic — checks AC coverage, naming conventions, and test-id integrity. Delegates spec-syntax checks to the stack's e2e-patterns or test-generator skill. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-ticket-validator.md) · ★1)
- **quorum-triage**: Decides how a unit of work enters the pipeline — the spec path when the problem is still open, the ticket path when it is bounded and clear — and how deep the product stages go. Writes origin and complexity into status.yml. Read-only: it classifies, it never drafts. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-orchestrator/agents/quorum-triage.md) · ★1)
- **quota-budget-auditor**: Prevents hidden SerpAPI/provider quota burn and verifies live discovery is explicit. ([mjmichaelware/job-hunter-pro](https://github.com/mjmichaelware/job-hunter-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/quota-budget-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
